AI agent skill exfiltrates GitHub token / env secrets via dynamic-context shell-out AIActions on Objectives High
Detects the credential-theft primitive Snyk and Datadog documented in malicious Claude Code skills: SKILL.md 'dynamic context' lines (the `!`command`` backtick syntax that runs before the model sees content) executing `gh auth token`, env/secret dumps or SSH/AWS key reads under an AI-agent parent, typically chained to a `curl -X POST --data-binary` exfil. Targets the article's headline claim that skills may be 'exfiltrating your credentials right now'. Rationale: Anchors on the exact credential-theft strings cited in the campaign reporting - `gh auth token`, env dumps (printenv/os.environ), SSH keys, ~/.aws/credentials, wallet.dat, browser Login Data - parented by the article's AI agents, with an ExfilContext flag for the chained `curl --data-binary` POST Datadog observed. Cross-checked against: • https://snyk.io/blog/toxicskills-malicious-ai-agent-skills-clawhub/ • https://securitylabs.datadoghq.com/articles/malicious-skills-supply-chain-risks-in-coding-agents-with-dynamic-context/ • https://www.trendmicro.com/en_us/research/26/b/openclaw-skills-used-to-distribute-atomic-macos-stealer.html
ATT&CKT1552.001 T1555.003 T1567.002
Data sourcesEndpoint.Processes
Expected false positives · estimated rate: medium — model-assessed, tune to your environment
  • Developer or CI legitimately running `gh auth token` to script a GitHub API call
  • A coding agent reading project .env / process.env as part of a normal build or test task
  • DevOps skill that references ~/.aws/credentials for an authorized deployment
  • Dotenv/secrets-loader libraries echoing variable names (not values) during debug
  • Password-manager or browser-sync tooling touching 'Login Data'
Suppression filter
| where not (ProcessCommandLine has_any ("gh auth status","gh auth login","gh auth setup-git") and ExfilContext == "read-only")
Response runbook
1) Triage: inspect the full command line and the ExfilContext flag — 'inline-exfil' (cred read chained to curl/POST/nc) is near-certain malicious; identify the SKILL.md in .claude/skills that drove it and check for `!`backtick`` dynamic-context lines. 2) Contain: revoke the exposed credential immediately (rotate the gh token, AWS access keys, SSH keys), isolate the host, and quarantine the offending skill. 3) Hunt: search egress for POSTs carrying the stolen data to non-corporate hosts, correlate with UC1 (marketplace download) to find the source skill, and sweep other dev endpoints for the same SKILL.md hash/skill ID.
DeviceProcessEvents
| where Timestamp > ago(30d)
| where InitiatingProcessFileName in~ ("node","node.exe","claude","claude.exe","cursor","cursor.exe","openclaw","openclaw.exe","bash","sh","zsh","cmd.exe","powershell.exe","pwsh")
| where ProcessCommandLine has_any ("gh auth token","printenv","process.env","os.environ","cat .env","type .env","id_rsa","id_ed25519",".aws/credentials",".aws\\credentials","wallet.dat","Login Data")
| where AccountName !endswith "$"
| extend ExfilContext = iff(ProcessCommandLine has_any ("curl","wget","Invoke-RestMethod","--data-binary","-X POST","nc "), "inline-exfil", "read-only")
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, InitiatingProcessFileName, FileName, ProcessCommandLine, ExfilContext, InitiatingProcessCommandLine
| order by Timestamp desc
ImProcessCreate
| where TimeGenerated > ago(30d)
| where ParentProcessName has_any ("node","claude","cursor","openclaw","bash","sh","zsh","cmd","powershell","pwsh")
| where ActingProcessCommandLine has_any ("gh auth token","printenv","process.env","os.environ","cat .env","type .env","id_rsa","id_ed25519",".aws/credentials",".aws\\credentials","wallet.dat","Login Data")
| extend ExfilContext = iff(ActingProcessCommandLine has_any ("curl","wget","Invoke-RestMethod","--data-binary","-X POST","nc "), "inline-exfil", "read-only")
| project TimeGenerated, DvcHostname, ActorUsername, ParentProcessName, ActingProcessName, ActingProcessCommandLine, ExfilContext
| order by TimeGenerated desc
title: AI Agent Skill Extracts gh Token / Env Secrets via Shell-Out
id: c4a7e9b2-5d18-4f33-9a60-2b8f1e7c0d55
description: Detects an AI coding agent (Claude Code / Cursor / OpenClaw) or its child shell running gh auth token, environment dumps, or SSH/AWS/wallet credential reads - the dynamic-context credential-theft behaviour Snyk and Datadog documented in malicious ClawHub skills.
references:
  - https://snyk.io/blog/toxicskills-malicious-ai-agent-skills-clawhub/
  - https://securitylabs.datadoghq.com/articles/malicious-skills-supply-chain-risks-in-coding-agents-with-dynamic-context/
author: SOC Detection Engineering
date: 2026/06/26
tags:
  - attack.t1552.001
  - attack.t1555.003
  - attack.credential_access
logsource:
  category: process_creation
detection:
  selection_parent:
    ParentImage|endswith:
      - '\node.exe'
      - '/node'
      - '\claude.exe'
      - '/claude'
      - '\cursor.exe'
      - '/cursor'
      - '\openclaw.exe'
      - '/openclaw'
  selection_cred:
    CommandLine|contains:
      - 'gh auth token'
      - 'printenv'
      - 'os.environ'
      - 'cat .env'
      - 'id_rsa'
      - 'id_ed25519'
      - '.aws/credentials'
      - '.aws\credentials'
      - 'wallet.dat'
      - 'Login Data'
  condition: selection_parent and selection_cred
level: high
source:windows.sysmon @EventID:1 (@ParentImage:(*\\node.exe OR *\\claude.exe OR *\\cursor.exe OR *\\openclaw.exe) OR @Image:(*\\node.exe OR *\\claude.exe OR *\\cursor.exe OR *\\openclaw.exe)) @CommandLine:(*"gh auth token"* OR *printenv* OR *os.environ* OR *id_rsa* OR *id_ed25519* OR *credentials* OR *wallet.dat* OR *"Login Data"*)
#event_simpleName=ProcessRollup2 ParentBaseFileName=/(node|claude|cursor|openclaw|bash|sh|zsh|cmd\.exe|powershell\.exe|pwsh)/i
| CommandLine=/(gh\s+auth\s+token|printenv|os\.environ|process\.env|cat\s+\.env|id_rsa|id_ed25519|\.aws[\/\\]credentials|wallet\.dat|Login Data)/i
| ExfilContext := if(regex("(curl|wget|Invoke-RestMethod|--data-binary|-X POST|nc )", field=CommandLine), then="inline-exfil", else="read-only")
| groupBy([aid, ComputerName, ParentBaseFileName, ExfilContext, CommandLine], function=count())
| sort(count, order=desc)

title: Obfuscated / Encoded PowerShell Execution
id: 7c8b2e10-4d4f-4a8c-9c1f-bf6e2c4a8d08
status: experimental
description: |
    PowerShell launched with -EncodedCommand, base64 strings, hidden
    window, no-profile, bypass policy, IEX / Invoke-Expression / Net
    .WebClient.DownloadString — combinations indicating evasion.
references:
    - https://attack.mitre.org/techniques/T1059/001/
    - https://attack.mitre.org/techniques/T1027/
author: Clankerusecase
date: 2026/05/02
tags:
    - attack.t1059.001
    - attack.t1027
    - attack.execution
    - attack.defense-evasion
logsource:
    category: process_creation
    product: windows
detection:
    selection_image:
        Image|endswith:
            - '\powershell.exe'
            - '\pwsh.exe'
    selection_args:
        CommandLine|contains:
            - '-enc '
            - 'EncodedCommand'
            - 'FromBase64String'
            - 'Invoke-Expression'
            - 'IEX('
            - 'DownloadString'
            - 'Net.WebClient'
            - '-w hidden'
            - '-WindowStyle Hidden'
            - '-nop'
            - '-NoProfile'
            - '-ep bypass'
            - '-ExecutionPolicy Bypass'
    condition: selection_image and selection_args
falsepositives:
    - DevOps tooling that legitimately uses encoded command for
      cross-shell escaping
level: high

Trusted vendor binary / installer launching unusual children Exploitation Medium

Data sourcesEndpoint.Processes DeviceProcessEvents

| where AccountName !endswith "$"
| where InitiatingProcessFileName in~ ("setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe")
| where FileName in~ ("powershell.exe","cmd.exe","rundll32.exe","regsvr32.exe","mshta.exe","wscript.exe","cscript.exe","wmic.exe","bitsadmin.exe")
| project Timestamp, DeviceName, AccountName, InitiatingProcessFileName, FileName, ProcessCommandLine
// Replace `"setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe"` with the vendor binaries observed in the
// article (matching shape with the Defender query).
SecurityEvent
| where TimeGenerated > ago(7d)
| where EventID == 4688
| where Account !endswith "$"
| where ParentProcessName has_any ("setup.exe","installer.exe","update.exe")
| where NewProcessName endswith "\powershell.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\cmd.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\rundll32.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\regsvr32.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\mshta.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\wscript.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\cscript.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\wmic.exe"
   or NewProcessName endswith "\bitsadmin.exe"
| project TimeGenerated, Computer, Account, ParentProcessName,
          NewProcessName, CommandLine
| order by TimeGenerated desc

title: Trusted Vendor Binary Launching Unusual Children
id: 7c8b2e10-4d4f-4a8c-9c1f-bf6e2c4a8d09
status: experimental
description: |
    A signed vendor binary (in the article's named-vendors list)
    spawning script-host or LOLBin children. Strongest signal when
    paired with a recent CVE / supply-chain advisory naming the same
    vendor.
references:
    - https://attack.mitre.org/techniques/T1195/
author: Clankerusecase
date: 2026/05/02
tags:
    - attack.t1195
    - attack.initial-access
logsource:
    category: process_creation
    product: windows
detection:
    selection_child:
        Image|endswith:
            - '\powershell.exe'
            - '\pwsh.exe'
            - '\cmd.exe'
            - '\rundll32.exe'
            - '\regsvr32.exe'
            - '\mshta.exe'
            - '\wscript.exe'
            - '\cscript.exe'
            - '\wmic.exe'
            - '\bitsadmin.exe'
    selection_parent_vendor:
        ParentImage|contains:
            - '\Program Files\'
            - '\Program Files (x86)\'
    condition: selection_child and selection_parent_vendor
falsepositives:
    - Many — installers and updaters legitimately spawn children. Pin
      the parent path to specific vendor binaries observed in the
      article before alerting.
level: medium
ENDPOINT

Critical

CISA KEV: CVE-2025-11953 — React Native Community CLI OS Command Injection Vulnerability

CISA KEV
2026-02-05 1 use case 0 techniques 2 kill-chain phases detected

React Native Community CLI contains an OS command injection vulnerability which could allow unauthenticated network attackers to send POST requests to the Metro Development Server and run arbitrary executables via a vulnerable endpoint exposed by the server. On Windows, attackers can also execute arbitrary shell commands with fully controlled arguments. Vendor: React Native Community, Product: CLI. Federal patch due: 2026-02-26.

CVEsCVE-2025-11953

Actions on Objectives
—
Reconnaissance. Attacker researches the target — OSINT, scanning, enumeration. → Asset exposure — vulnerability matches article CVE(s)
Weaponization. Coupling exploit code with deliverable payload (e.g. weaponized PDF/Office doc, trojanized installer).
Delivery. Transmitting the weapon (phishing email, USB, watering hole, drive-by, malicious ad).
Exploitation. Triggering the exploit — vulnerability/macro/trust abuse to execute code.
Installation. Establishing persistence on the host — backdoor, service, scheduled task, registry key.
Command & Control. Beacon to attacker infrastructure for control and tasking.
Actions on Objectives. Lateral movement, credential dumping, data theft, ransomware, sabotage.
Asset exposure — vulnerability matches article CVE(s) Reconnaissance High
Match vulnerability scanner output to the CVE(s) named in the article.
ATT&CKT1190
Data sourcesVulnerabilities DeviceInfo DeviceTvmSoftwareVulnerabilities
// TVM tables are daily snapshots — pin a 1-day window so the engine
// partitions on Timestamp instead of scanning the full retention.
DeviceTvmSoftwareVulnerabilities
| where Timestamp > ago(1d)
| where CveId in~ ("CVE-2025-11953")
| join kind=inner (DeviceInfo | where Timestamp > ago(1d)) on DeviceId
| project DeviceName, OSPlatform, CveId, VulnerabilitySeverityLevel, RecommendedSecurityUpdate
| order by VulnerabilitySeverityLevel asc, DeviceName asc

// Sentinel has no native TVM table. If MDE→Sentinel streaming is enabled,
// `DeviceTvmSoftwareVulnerabilities` is available. Otherwise this UC has
// no Sentinel translation — track via Update / Heartbeat OS-version
// gaps as a coarse approximation.
DeviceTvmSoftwareVulnerabilities
| where TimeGenerated > ago(1d)
| where CveId in~ ("CVE-2025-11953")
| join kind=inner (DeviceInfo | where TimeGenerated > ago(1d)) on DeviceId
| project DeviceName, OSPlatform, CveId,
          VulnerabilitySeverityLevel, RecommendedSecurityUpdate
| order by VulnerabilitySeverityLevel asc, DeviceName asc
